- **unity-perf-audit**: Use when the user wants a static performance review of a Unity project's C# code, without running the game - scanning scripts for CPU, GPU, and memory anti-patterns (GetComponent or Find in Update, allocations and LINQ in hot paths, Camera.main, string concatenation, event leaks, runtime Instantiate, uncached WaitForSeconds) and producing a scored report with file:line locations and before/after fixes. Triggers include "perf audit", "performance audit", "audit my Unity code for performance", "optimize performance", "performance anti-patterns", "GC allocations in Update", "why is my code slow"… ([luckyfried/code-config-tools](https://github.com/luckyfried/code-config-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/unity-perf-audit) · ★1)
